# Removal stage 3 — test statements removed, ported or restored

One line per change to a test script made while removing Flame Tests, Inspections, Venue
Assessments / site visits, the Assessments engine, the customer portal and the service engines
(see docs/removal-log.md, "Stage 3"). Format:

  <file>:<first line>-<last line> [<why>] <first 140 characters of the statement>

Line numbers are the file's at the time of that pass, not today's. Reasons:

  import / import-member   an import of a deleted module, or one name from it (also one name
                           destructured from `await import(...)`)
  dead-path <path>         a string naming a deleted source file (a source-pin read)
  dead-path element <path> one deleted path removed from an array of paths that are otherwise live
  dead-dir loop / element  a loop over removed screen directories ("flame-tests", "inspections", …)
                           whose file paths are built from a template; the whole loop when every
                           directory is gone, else only the dead entries
  TS####                   the statement no longer compiled once the above were gone
  prop                     one property removed from an object literal
  runtime crash            a top-level statement that read or grepped removed code and stopped the run
  runtime FAIL <message>   the assertion failed because it pinned removed code or text
  manual: ...              done by hand — a block or suite deleted, an assertion updated to the new
                           source or copy, a test ported to a kept example, a suite dropped from or
                           added to the async chain, an import re-pointed at a helper's new module

Whole files deleted (not listed line by line): scripts/test-assessments.ts (2,364 lines — the
Assessments engine) and scripts/test-va-notes.ts (3,038 lines — Venue Assessment notes). Their
suites (assessmentChecks, assessmentDbChecks, vaNotesChecks, vaNotesDbChecks) were dropped from
the harness chain by name. The csv, photo-resize and short-code checks from test-assessments.ts
were ported to scripts/test-moved-helpers.ts (new).
